Of 64 previous decisions, 34 classifications changed and 30 were retained; four holds gained qualified support and six colors were removed. Native operations, parameter/result declarations, typed structures, invocation progress/correlation, declared support and provenance can be useful information without exact Integrated Sensor Architecture (ISA) equivalence or evidence from a live device. Generic files, references, properties and labels do not by themselves establish observation-media associations, occurrence identity, equipment operating condition or changeable equipment names.

**Meaning retained:** a message identifier is distinct from a message-type identifier, an underlying occurrence identifier and a response-correlation rule. Component/software-instance attribution is useful without automatically proving authorship authentication; an audit relay is not automatically the original producer. Writer priority does not become individual-message priority, an update deadline does not become payload expiry, and a log timestamp does not become transmission time. Vendor declarations, standard lifecycle standing and prescribed encoding choices remain narrow candidates, without a generic assertion schema, ISA extension-acceptance equivalence, negotiated connection or installed-case fact.

## Handover and future execution discipline

One owner `Proceed` covers **one** specifically authorized and verified step, never the whole C1–C4 plan. At that step, inspect original source records as needed, retain stable IDs and native meanings, progressively update **one** canonical JSON + both CSV/Markdown views, and refresh the **same Excel** at its defined substantive checkpoints; label exact lag when the workbook is older. Publish concise actual evidence/status updates in **existing** PITON/plan/README and verify branch/HEAD, committed file readback and saved cells when changed. Do not create duplicate PITONs, redundant research packets or per-batch publishers, and do not turn a cleanup into source research, donor ranking or model adoption. At handover, report verified branch/HEAD, work actually completed, source holds, canonical comparison/Excel state, owner decisions and exactly one next action.

## Donor-by-donor source rigor and eventual reconciliation — controlling practice

The 40-step cross-donor plan is a **sequence of source families and owner checkpoints**, not proof that each donor can be adequately audited in exactly one conversation. ISA's 22-delivery correction remedied previously missed source coverage; subsequent donors should receive **source-native coverage assessment on entry**, with exact original editions, document structure, term inventory/relevance, prior verified findings, unavailable-text holds and an honest workload size. Use an appropriately scoped donor-specific plan only if necessary; propose extra bounded deliveries to the owner rather than silently multiplying them or forcing a cursory source audit into one prompt. Initial donor review and repair of prior errors are different activities. The Open Group SOSA must not be confused with the already reviewed W3C SOSA/SSN.

For every reviewed term, preserve a stable source ID and edition/clause locator, native kind and role, candidate DIM row or supporting-only disposition, **DIRECT/BROADER/NARROWER/PARTIAL/RELATED/CONFLICT/NO ESTABLISHED/UNREVIEWED** standing, a negative-equivalence caveat and case evidence where warranted. A single source may contribute multiple qualifiers to one candidate; several standards may express one semantic idea differently; not every source term deserves a final class or field. Preserve ISA-only terms without treating blank other-donor cells as absence. Update the one canonical source master as evidence is earned; explicit DIM content adoption remains separate.

The existing plan already contains the reconciliation checkpoints: Step24 **focused owner adoption of sufficiently ready, meaningful portions**, Steps35–39 **concept clustering, alias/duplicate/split and overload decisions, supported donor ranking, diagrams, source-case challenge and quality verification**, and Step40 **coherent named-version approval**. Reconciliation must be evidence-first and track every merge/split/hold with stable lineage; it is not simple text matching, source-count voting or top-level class proliferation. Record tensions as each donor is studied and resolve them at the appropriate established checkpoint. No additional research campaign, future donor correction, workbook re-layout, or DIM decision is authorized by Step16 acceptance alone.

## Retained master-integration and adoption record (historical counts)

## What was agreed in the latest discussion

The owner asked when the accumulated ISA work would enter the master, agreed to bring it into the working master now, asked when substantive DIM adoption should occur, and authorized proceeding with the integration and recovery/progress update.

These are separate decisions:
1. **Master inclusion:** verified donor meanings, corrections and candidate contributions belong in the working comparison now. A source-backed correction is not held outside the master pending final model approval.
2. **DIM adoption:** decide what belongs in the Domain Information Model, its meaning and representation, explicitly and with cross-donor support. This publication does not adopt every source row as a model field or class.

**Agreed adoption approach:** at broader cross-donor Step24, bring ready coherent portions forward for explicit owner decisions. Readiness requires a clear meaning and subject, a demonstrated need within scope, comparison of relevant alternatives, and a concrete representation/example. These are questions answered by existing research, not four new prompts per field. Do not require majority-donor agreement or exhaustive rereading of all182 artifacts.

Keep major architecture open to credible donor challenges through the remaining reviews. Reconcile structure and diagrams and test source cases in broader Steps35–39, then seek approval of a coherent named DIM version at Step40. An accepted portion is recorded as accepted for that version, not indefinitely proposed. Later evidence revises the affected decision specifically; it does not reopen all completed work. Source uncertainty remains local.
